"logger.debug("], "content": "          \"content\": \"# Python Web异步编程技术系统性调研 - 实施指南报告\\n\\n> 版本：V4.0  \\n> 类型：实施指南报告  \\n> 目标受众：技术实施团队、开发工程师  \\n> 生成时间：2026-07-11\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 目录\\n\\n1. [实施准备](#1-实施准备)\\n2. [技术栈搭建指南](#2-技术栈搭建指南)\\n3. [代码规范与最佳实践](#3-代码规范与最佳实践)\\n4. [性能优化指南](#4-性能优化指南)\\n5. [监控与运维指南](#5-监控与运维指南)\\n6. [测试策略](#6-测试策略)\\n7. [部署指南](#7-部署指南)\\n8. [常见问题与解决方案](#8-常见问题与解决方案)\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 1. 实施准备\\n\\n### 1.1 环境要求\\n\\n#### Python版本\\n```bash\\n# 推荐Python 3.11+\\npython --version  # Python 3.11.0+\\n\\n# 安装Python 3.11\\n# Ubuntu/Debian\\nsudo apt update\\nsudo apt install python3.11 python3.11-venv python3.11-pip\\n\\n# macOS\\nbrew install python@3.11\\n\\n# Windows\\n# 从 https://www.python.org/downloads/ 下载\\n```\\n\\n#### 依赖管理工具\\n```bash\\n# 使用uv（推荐）\\npip install uv\\n\\n# 或使用pip + virtualenv\\npip install virtualenv\\n```\\n\\n### 1.2 技术栈选择决策\\n\\n根据业务场景选择技术组合：\\n\\n```\\n场景一：高性能API服务\\n├── Web框架：FastAPI\\n├── 数据库驱动：asyncpg\\n├── 任务队列：Dramatiq\\n└── 服务器：Uvicorn\\n\\n场景二：企业级应用\\n├── Web框架：FastAPI\\n├── 数据库驱动：SQLAlchemy async\\n├── 任务队列：Celery\\n└── 服务器：Uvicorn\\n\\n场景三：快速原型开发\\n├── Web框架：FastAPI\\n├── 数据库驱动：databases\\n├── 任务队列：ARQ\\n└── 服务器：Uvicorn\\n\\n场景四：Flask应用迁移\\n├── Web框架：Quart\\n├── 数据库驱动：asyncpg\\n├── 任务队列：Dramatiq\\n└── 服务器：Uvicorn\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 2. 技术栈搭建指南\\n\\n### 2.1 FastAPI项目搭建\\n\\n#### 项目结构\\n```\\nproject/\\n├── app/\\n│   ├── __init__.py\\n│   ├── main.py          # 应用入口\\n│   ├── config.py        # 配置管理\\n│   ├── dependencies.py  # 依赖注入\\n│   ├── models/          # 数据模型\\n│   │   ├── __init__.py\\n│   │   └── user.py\\n│   ├── routers/         # 路由模块\\n│   │   ├── __init__.py\\n│   │   ├── user.py\\n│   │   └── item.py\\n│   ├── services/        # 业务逻辑\\n│   │   ├── __init__.py\\n│   │   └── user_service.py\\n│   ├── repositories/    # 数据访问层\\n│   │   ├── __init__.py\\n│   │   └── user_repository.py\\n│   └── schemas/         # Pydantic模式\\n│       ├── __init__.py\\n│       └── user.py\\n├── tests/               # 测试代码\\n│   ├── __init__.py\\n│   ├── conftest.py\\n│   └── test_user.py\\n├── requirements.txt     # 依赖文件\\n├── Dockerfile           # Docker配置\\n└── docker-compose.yml   # 容器编排\\n```\\n\\n#### 安装依赖\\n```bash\\n# 创建虚拟环境\\nuv venv\\nsource .venv/bin/activate  # Linux/macOS\\n# .venv\\\\Scripts\\\\activate   # Windows\\n\\n# 安装核心依赖\\nuv pip install fastapi uvicorn[standard] pydantic python-multipart\\n\\n# 安装数据库驱动\\nuv pip install asyncpg sqlalchemy databases\\n\\n# 安装任务队列\\nuv pip install dramatiq redis\\n\\n# 安装其他工具\\nuv pip install pytest pytest-asyncio httpx python-dotenv\\n```\\n\\n#### 配置文件\\n```python\\n# app/config.py\\nfrom pydantic_settings import BaseSettings\\nfrom functools import lru_cache\\n\\nclass Settings(BaseSettings):\\n    # 应用配置\\n    app_name: str = \\\"My Async App\\\"\\n    app_version: str = \\\"1.0.0\\\"\\n    debug: bool = False\\n    \\n    # 数据库配置\\n    database_url: str = \\\"postgresql+asyncpg://user:pass@localhost/dbname\\\"\\n    database_pool_size: int = 20\\n    database_max_overflow: int = 10\\n    \\n    # Redis配置\\n    redis_url: str = \\\"redis://localhost:6379/0\\\"\\n    \\n    # 任务队列配置\\n    task_queue_max_workers: int = 4\\n    \\n    class Config:\\n        env_file = \\\".env\\\"\\n\\n@lru_cache\\ndef get_settings():\\n    return Settings()\\n```\\n\\n#### 应用入口\\n```python\\n# app/main.py\\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI\\nfrom app.config import get_settings\\nfrom app.routers import user, item\\n\\nsettings = get_settings()\\n\\napp = FastAPI(\\n    title=settings.app_name,\\n    version=settings.app_version,\\n    description=\\\"Python Web异步编程技术演示应用\\\"\\n)\\n\\n# 注册路由\\napp.include_router(user.router, prefix=\\\"/users\\\", tags=[\\\"用户\\\"])\\napp.include_router(item.router, prefix=\\\"/items\\\", tags=[\\\"商品\\\"])\\n\\n@app.get(\\\"/\\\")\\nasync def root():\\n    return {\\\"message\\\": \\\"Welcome to the API!\\\", \\\"version\\\": settings.app_version}\\n\\n@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")\\nasync def health_check():\\n    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\"}\\n```\\n\\n### 2.2 asyncpg数据库配置\\n\\n#### 连接池配置\\n```python\\n# app/database.py\\nimport asyncpg\\nfrom app.config import get_settings\\n\\nsettings = get_settings()\\n\\nclass Database:\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        self.pool = None\\n    \\n    async def connect(self):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"建立数据库连接池\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.pool = await asyncpg.create_pool(\\n            dsn=settings.database_url,\\n            min_size=settings.database_pool_size,\\n            max_size=settings.database_pool_size + settings.database_max_overflow,\\n            max_queries=50000,\\n            max_inactive_connection_lifetime=300.0,\\n            command_timeout=60\\n        )\\n    \\n    async def disconnect(self):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"关闭数据库连接池\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if self.pool:\\n            await self.pool.close()\\n    \\n    async def get_connection(self):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"获取数据库连接\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if not self.pool:\\n            await self.connect()\\n        return self.pool\\n\\n# 全局数据库实例\\ndb = Database()\\n\\n# 应用生命周期事件\\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI\\n\\nasync def startup():\\n    await db.connect()\\n\\nasync def shutdown():\\n    await db.disconnect()\\n\\ndef setup_database(app: FastAPI):\\n    app.add_event_handler(\\\"startup\\\", startup)\\n    app.add_event_handler(\\\"shutdown\\\", shutdown)\\n```\\n\\n#### 数据访问层示例\\n```python\\n# app/repositories/user_repository.py\\nimport asyncpg\\nfrom app.database import db\\nfrom typing import Optional, List\\n\\nclass UserRepository:\\n    async def create_table(self):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"创建用户表\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        async with db.get_connection().acquire() as conn:\\n            await conn.execute(\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (\\n                    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,\\n                    name VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,\\n                    email VARCHAR(100) UNIQUE NOT NULL,\\n                    age INTEGER,\\n                    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW()\\n                )\\n            \\\"\\\"\\\")\\n    \\n    async def get_user(self, user_id: int) -> Optional[dict]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"获取用户\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        async with db.get_connection().acquire() as conn:\\n            row = await conn.fetchrow(\\n                \\\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = $1\\\",\\n                user_id\\n            )\\n            return dict(row) if row else None\\n    \\n    async def get_users(self, skip: int = 0, limit: int = 100) -> List[dict]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"获取用户列表\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        async with db.get_connection().acquire() as conn:\\n            rows = await conn.fetch(\\n                \\\"SELECT * FROM users ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT $1 OFFSET $2\\\",\\n                limit, skip\\n            )\\n            return [dict(row) for row in rows]\\n    \\n    async def create_user(self, name: str, email: str, age: Optional[int] = None) -> dict:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"创建用户\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        async with db.get_connection().acquire() as conn:\\n            row = await conn.fetchrow(\\n                \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n                INSERT INTO users (name, email, age) \\n                VALUES ($1, $2, $3) \\n                RETURNING *\\n                \\\"\\\"\\\",\\n                name, email, age\\n            )\\n            return dict(row)\\n    \\n    async def update_user(self, user_id: int, **kwargs) -> Optional[dict]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"更新用户\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if not kwargs:\\n            return None\\n        \\n        set_clause = \\\", \\\".join([f\\\"{key} = ${idx}\\\" for idx, key in enumerate(kwargs.keys(), 1)])\\n        values = list(kwargs.values())\\n        \\n        async with db.get_connection().acquire() as conn:\\n            row = await conn.fetchrow(\\n                f\\\"UPDATE users SET {set_clause} WHERE id = ${len(values) + 1} RETURNING *\\\",\\n                *values, user_id\\n            )\\n            return dict(row) if row else None\\n    \\n    async def delete_user(self, user_id: int) -> bool:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"删除用户\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        async with db.get_connection().acquire() as conn:\\n            result = await conn.execute(\\n                \\\"DELETE FROM users WHERE id = $1\\\",\\n                user_id\\n            )\\n            return \\\"DELETE 1\\\" in result\\n\\n# 全局实例\\nuser_repository = UserRepository()\\n```\\n\\n### 2.3 Dramatiq任务队列配置\\n\\n#### 配置\\n```python\\n# app/tasks/config.py\\nimport dramatiq\\nimport redis\\n\\n# Redis连接\\nredis_client = redis.Redis(host='localhost', port=6379, db=0)\\n\\n# 创建broker\\nbroker = dramatiq.Broker(broker=redis_client)\\n\\n# 创建应用\\napp = dramatiq.Broker(broker=broker)\\n```\\n\\n#### 任务定义\\n```python\\n# app/tasks/user_tasks.py\\nfrom app.tasks.config import app\\nimport logging\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n@app.actor(max_retries=3, min_backoff=60_000, max_backoff=600_000)\\ndef send_welcome_email(user_id: int, email: str):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"发送欢迎邮件\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    logger.info(f\\\"Sending welcome email to user {user_id}\\\")\\n    # 实际发送邮件逻辑\\n    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"sent\\\", \\\"user_id\\\": user_id}\\n\\n@app.actor\\nasync def process_user_data(user_id: int):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"处理用户数据（异步任务）\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    logger.info(f\\\"Processing data for user {user_id}\\\")\\n    # 异步处理逻辑\\n    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"processed\\\", \\\"user_id\\\": user_id}\\n\\n@app.actor\\ndef generate_report(report_type: str, params: dict):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"生成报告\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    logger.info(f\\\"Generating {report_type} report\\\")\\n    # 生成报告逻辑\\n    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"generated\\\", \\\"type\\\": report_type}\\n```\\n\\n#### 启动Worker\\n```python\\n# app/tasks/worker.py\\nfrom dramatiq import Worker\\nfrom app.tasks.config import broker\\nimport logging\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\ndef start_worker():\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"启动任务队列Worker\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    worker = Worker(broker=broker, worker_timeout=300_000)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Starting Dramatiq worker...\\\")\\n    worker.start()\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    start_worker()\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 3. 代码规范与最佳实践\\n\\n### 3.1 异步编程最佳实践\\n\\n#### 正确使用async/await\\n```python\\n# ✅ 正确：使用async/await\\nasync def fetch_data():\\n    await asyncio.sleep(1)\\n    return {\\\"data\\\": \\\"result\\\"}\\n\\n# ❌ 错误：在async函数中使用同步阻塞操作\\nasync def bad_example():\\n    time.sleep(1)  # 阻塞事件循环\\n    return {\\\"data\\\": \\\"result\\\"}\\n```\\n\\n#### 并发操作\\n```python\\n# ✅ 正确：使用asyncio.gather并发执行\\nasync def fetch_multiple_data():\\n    tasks = [\\n        fetch_data(\\\"url1\\\"),\\n        fetch_data(\\\"url2\\\"),\\n        fetch_data(\\\"url3\\\")\\n    ]\\n    results = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)\\n    return results\\n\\n# ❌ 错误：顺序执行\\nasync def bad_example():\\n    result1 = await fetch_data(\\\"url1\\\")\\n    result2 = await fetch_data(\\\"url2\\\")\\n    result3 = await fetch_data(\\\"url3\\\")\\n    return [result1, result2, result3]\\n```\\n\\n#### 异常处理\\n```python\\n# ✅ 正确：使用try-except处理异常\\nasync def safe_operation():\\n    try:\\n        await risky_operation()\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        logger.error(f\\\"Operation failed: {e}\\\")\\n        raise\\n\\n# ✅ 正确：使用asyncio.wait_for设置超时\\nasync def operation_with_timeout():\\n    try:\\n        result = await asyncio.wait_for(\\n            long_operation(),\\n            timeout=30.0\\n        )\\n        return result\\n    except asyncio.TimeoutError:\\n        logger.error(\\\"Operation timed out\\\")\\n        raise\\n```\\n\\n### 3.2 数据库操作最佳实践\\n\\n#### 使用连接池\\n```python\\n# ✅ 正确：使用连接池\\nasync def get_user(user_id: int):\\n    async with db.get_connection().acquire() as conn:\\n        row = await conn.fetchrow(\\\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = $1\\\", user_id)\\n        return dict(row) if row else None\\n\\n# ❌ 错误：每次创建新连接\\nasync def bad_example(user_id: int):\\n    conn = await asyncpg.connect(dsn=DATABASE_URL)\\n    try:\\n        row = await conn.fetchrow(\\\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = $1\\\", user_id)\\n        return dict(row) if row else None\\n    finally:\\n        await conn.close()\\n```\\n\\n#### 事务处理\\n```python\\n# ✅ 正确：使用事务\\nasync def transfer_money(from_id: int, to_id: int, amount: float):\\n    async with db.get_connection().acquire() as conn:\\n        async with conn.transaction():\\n            # 扣款\\n            await conn.execute(\\n                \\\"UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance - $1 WHERE id = $2\\\",\\n                amount, from_id\\n            )\\n            # 入账\\n            await conn.execute(\\n                \\\"UPDATE accounts SET balance = balance + $1 WHERE id = $2\\\",\\n                amount, to_id\\n            )\\n            # 自动提交，异常时自动回滚\\n```\\n\\n#### 批量操作\\n```python\\n# ✅ 正确：使用executemany批量插入\\nasync def bulk_insert_users(users: List[dict]):\\n    async with db.get_connection().acquire() as conn:\\n        await conn.executemany(\\n            \\\"INSERT INTO users (name, email, age) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)\\\",\\n            [(u[\\\"name\\\"], u[\\\"email\\\"], u.get(\\\"age\\\")) for u in users]\\n        )\\n```\\n\\n### 3.3 任务队列最佳实践\\n\\n#### 任务设计\\n```python\\n# ✅ 正确：任务函数保持简单、幂等\\n@app.actor(max_retries=3)\\ndef process_order(order_id: int):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"处理订单\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # 获取订单\\n    order = get_order(order_id)\\n    # 处理逻辑\\n    process_order_logic(order)\\n    # 返回结果\\n    return {\\\"status\\\": \\\"processed\\\", \\\"order_id\\\": order_id}\\n\\n# ❌ 错误：任务函数过于复杂\\n@app.actor\\ndef complex_task():\\n    # 不要在这里做复杂的业务逻辑\\n    # 不要在这里访问数据库（除非必要）\\n    # 不要在这里做网络请求（除非必要）\\n    pass\\n```\\n\\n#### 错误处理\\n```python\\n# ✅ 正确：使用max_retries和异常处理\\n@app.actor(max_retries=3, min_backoff=60_000)\\ndef risky_task(data: dict):\\n    try:\\n        # 执行任务\\n        result = do_risky_operation(data)\\n        return result\\n    except TemporaryError as e:\\n        # 临时错误，重试\\n        raise\\n    except PermanentError as e:\\n        # 永久错误，不重试\\n        logger.error(f\\\"Permanent error: {e}\\\")\\n        raise\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 4. 性能优化指南\\n\\n### 4.1 数据库性能优化\\n\\n#### 连接池配置\\n```python\\n# 根据业务量调整连接池大小\\npool = await asyncpg.create_pool(\\n    dsn=DATABASE_URL,\\n    min_size=5,           # 最小连接数（根据并发量调整）\\n    max_size=20,          # 最大连接数（根据数据库容量调整）\\n    max_queries=50000,    # 单连接最大查询数（防止连接老化）\\n    max_inactive_connection_lifetime=300.0,  # 连接最大空闲时间\\n    command_timeout=60    # 命令超时\\n)\\n```\\n\\n#### 查询优化\\n```python\\n# ✅ 正确：使用参数化查询\\nasync def get_user_by_email(email: str):\\n    async with db.get_connection().acquire() as conn:\\n        row = await conn.fetchrow(\\n            \\\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = $1\\\",\\n            email\\n        )\\n        return dict(row) if row else None\\n\\n# ❌ 错误：字符串拼接（SQL注入风险）\\nasync def bad_example(email: str):\\n    async with db.get_connection().acquire() as conn:\\n        row = await conn.fetchrow(\\n            f\\\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE email = '{email}'\\\"\\n        )\\n        return dict(row) if row else None\\n```\\n\\n#### 索引优化\\n```sql\\n-- 创建常用查询字段的索引\\nCREATE INDEX idx_users_email ON users(email);\\nCREATE INDEX idx_users_created_at ON users(created_at DESC);\\nCREATE INDEX idx_orders_user_id ON orders(user_id);\\n```\\n\\n### 4.2 应用性能优化\\n\\n#### 缓存策略\\n```python\\n# 使用Redis缓存\\nimport redis\\nfrom functools import lru_cache\\n\\nredis_client = redis.Redis(host='localhost', port=6379, db=1)\\n\\nasync def get_user_with_cache(user_id: int):\\n    # 先从缓存获取\\n    cached = redis_client.get(f\\\"user:{user_id}\\\")\\n    if cached:\\n        return json.loads(cached)\\n    \\n    # 缓存未命中，从数据库获取\\n    user = await user_repository.get_user(user_id)\\n    if user:\\n        # 写入缓存，设置过期时间\\n        redis_client.setex(\\n            f\\\"user:{user_id}\\\",\\n            3600,  # 1小时过期\\n            json.dumps(user)\\n        )\\n    return user\\n```\\n\\n#### 异步操作优化\\n```python\\n# 使用asyncio.gather并发执行多个独立操作\\nasync def get_user_with_details(user_id: int):\\n    # 并发获取用户信息和订单\\n    user_task = user_repository.get_user(user_id)\\n    orders_task = order_repository.get_user_orders(user_id)\\n    \\n    user, orders = await asyncio.gather(user_task, orders_task)\\n    \\n    return {\\n        \\\"user\\\": user,\\n        \\\"orders\\\": orders\\n    }\\n```\\n\\n### 4.3 服务器性能优化\\n\\n#### Uvicorn配置\\n```bash\\n# 启动命令\\nuvicorn app.main:app \\\\\\n  --host 0.0.0.0 \\\\\\n  --port 8000 \\\\\\n  --workers 4 \\\\\\n  --loop uvloop \\\\\\n  --http httptools\\n```\\n\\n#### 生产环境配置\\n```python\\n# 使用Gunicorn + Uvicorn\\nfrom gunicorn.app.wsgiapp import run\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    run(\\n        \\\"app.main:app\\\",\\n        bind=\\\"0.0.0.0:8000\\\",\\n        workers=4,\\n        worker_class=\\\"uvicorn.workers.UvicornWorker\\\",\\n        timeout=120,\\n        keepalive=5\\n    )\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 5. 监控与运维指南\\n\\n### 5.1 应用监控\\n\\n#### 健康检查端点\\n```python\\n# app/main.py\\n@app.get(\\\"/health\\\")\\nasync def health_check():\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"健康检查端点\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    health = {\\n        \\\"status\\\": \\\"healthy\\\",\\n        \\\"timestamp\\\": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),\\n        \\\"version\\\": settings.app_version\\n    }\\n    \\n    # 检查数据库连接\\n    try:\\n        async with db.get_connection().acquire() as conn:\\n            await conn.fetchval(\\\"SELECT 1\\\")\\n        health[\\\"database\\\"] = \\\"healthy\\\"\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        health[\\\"database\\\"] = f\\\"unhealthy: {str(e)}\\\"\\n        health[\\\"status\\\"] = \\\"unhealthy\\\"\\n    \\n    # 检查Redis连接\\n    try:\\n        redis_client.ping()\\n        health[\\\"redis\\\"] = \\\"healthy\\\"\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        health[\\\"redis\\\"] = f\\\"unhealthy: {str(e)}\\\"\\n        health[\\\"status\\\"] = \\\"unhealthy\\\"\\n    \\n    return health\\n```\\n\\n#### 性能监控\\n```python\\n# 使用Prometheus + Grafana\\nfrom prometheus_client import Counter, Histogram, generate_latest\\nfrom fastapi import Request\\nimport time\\n\\n# 定义指标\\nREQUEST_COUNT = Counter(\\\"http_requests_total\\\", \\\"Total HTTP requests\\\", [\\\"method\\\", \\\"endpoint\\\", \\\"status\\\"])\\nREQUEST_LATENCY = Histogram(\\\"http_request_duration_seconds\\\", \\\"HTTP request latency\\\")\\n\\n@app.middleware(\\\"http\\\")\\nasync def metrics_middleware(request: Request, call_next):\\n    start_time = time.time()\\n    \\n    response = await call_next(request)\\n    \\n    # 记录指标\\n    REQUEST_COUNT.labels(\\n        method=request.method,\\n        endpoint=request.url.path,\\n        status=response.status_code\\n    ).inc()\\n    \\n    REQUEST_LATENCY.observe(time.time() - start_time)\\n    \\n    return response\\n\\n@app.get(\\\"/metrics\\\")\\nasync def metrics():\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Prometheus指标端点\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return Response(content=generate_latest(), media_type=\\\"text/plain\\\")\\n```\\n\\n### 5.2 日志配置\\n\\n#### 日志配置\\n```python\\n# app/logging.py\\nimport logging\\nfrom logging.handlers import RotatingFileHandler\\n\\ndef setup_logging():\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"配置日志\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    logging.basicConfig(\\n        level=logging.INFO,\\n        format=\\\"%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s\\\",\\n        handlers=[\\n            RotatingFileHandler(\\n                \\\"logs/app.log\\\",\\n                maxBytes=10*1024*1024,  # 10MB\\n                backupCount=5\\n            ),\\n            logging.StreamHandler()\\n        ]\\n    )\\n    \\n    # 设置第三方库日志级别\\n    logging.getLogger(\\\"uvicorn\\\").setLevel(logging.INFO)\\n    logging.getLogger(\\\"asyncpg\\\").setLevel(logging.WARNING)\\n\\n# 使用\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n```\\n\\n### 5.3 任务队列监控\\n\\n#### Dramatiq Dashboard\\n```bash\\n# 启动Dramatiq Dashboard\\ndramatiq-dashboard \\\\\\n  --broker redis://localhost:6379/0 \\\\\\n  --host 0.0.0.0 \\\\\\n  --port 9191\\n```\\n\\n#### Celery Flower\\n```bash\\n# 启动Celery Flower\\ncelery -A app.tasks flower \\\\\\n  --broker=redis://localhost:6379/0 \\\\\\n  --address=0.0.0.0 \\\\\\n  --port=5555\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 6. 测试策略\\n\\n### 6.1 单元测试\\n\\n#### 测试依赖\\n```bash\\npip install pytest pytest-asyncio httpx\\n```\\n\\n#### 测试示例\\n```python\\n# tests/test_user.py\\nimport pytest\\nfrom httpx import AsyncClient\\nfrom app.main import app\\nfrom app.database import db\\n\\n@pytest.fixture\\nasync def client():\\n    async with AsyncClient(app=app, base_url=\\\"http://test\\\") as ac:\\n        yield ac\\n\\n@pytest.fixture\\nasync def setup_database():\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"设置测试数据库\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    await db.connect()\\n    await user_repository.create_table()\\n    yield\\n    await db.disconnect()\\n\\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\\nasync def test_create_user(client, setup_database):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"测试创建用户\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    response = await client.post(\\n        \\\"/users\\\",\\n        json={\\\"name\\\": \\\"John\\\", \\\"email\\\": \\\"john@example.com\\\", \\\"age\\\": 30}\\n    )\\n    \\n    assert response.status_code == 201\\n    data = response.json()\\n    assert data[\\\"name\\\"] == \\\"John\\\"\\n    assert data[\\\"email\\\"] == \\\"john@example.com\\\"\\n    assert \\\"id\\\" in data\\n\\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\\nasync def test_get_user(client, setup_database):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"测试获取用户\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # 先创建用户\\n    create_response = await client.post(\\n        \\\"/users\\\",\\n        json={\\\"name\\\": \\\"John\\\", \\\"email\\\": \\\"john@example.com\\\"}\\n    )\\n    user_id = create_response.json()[\\\"id\\\"]\\n    \\n    # 获取用户\\n    response = await client.get(f\\\"/users/{user_id}\\\")\\n    \\n    assert response.status_code == 200\\n    data = response.json()\\n    assert data[\\\"id\\\"] == user_id\\n    assert data[\\\"name\\\"] == \\\"John\\\"\\n```\\n\\n### 6.2 集成测试\\n\\n#### 数据库集成测试\\n```python\\n# tests/test_database.py\\nimport pytest\\nimport asyncpg\\nfrom app.database import db\\n\\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\\nasync def test_database_connection():\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"测试数据库连接\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    await db.connect()\\n    \\n    async with db.get_connection().acquire() as conn:\\n        result = await conn.fetchval(\\\"SELECT 1\\\")\\n        assert result == 1\\n    \\n    await db.disconnect()\\n\\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\\nasync def test_user_repository():\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"测试用户仓库\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    await db.connect()\\n    await user_repository.create_table()\\n    \\n    # 创建用户\\n    user = await user_repository.create_user(\\\"John\\\", \\\"john@example.com\\\", 30)\\n    assert user[\\\"name\\\"] == \\\"John\\\"\\n    \\n    # 获取用户\\n    fetched = await user_repository.get_user(user[\\\"id\\\"])\\n    assert fetched[\\\"name\\\"] == \\\"John\\\"\\n    \\n    # 更新用户\\n    updated = await user_repository.update_user(user[\\\"id\\\"], name=\\\"Jane\\\")\\n    assert updated[\\\"name\\\"] == \\\"Jane\\\"\\n    \\n    # 删除用户\\n    deleted = await user_repository.delete_user(user[\\\"id\\\"])\\n    assert deleted is True\\n    \\n    # 验证删除\\n    fetched = await user_repository.get_user(user[\\\"id\\\"])\\n    assert fetched is None\\n    \\n    await db.disconnect()\\n```\\n\\n### 6.3 性能测试\\n\\n#### 使用Locust进行性能测试\\n```python\\n# tests/performance/locustfile.py\\nfrom locust import HttpUser, task, between\\n\\nclass UserUser(HttpUser):\\n    wait_time = between(1, 3)\\n    \\n    @task\\n    def get_user(self):\\n        self.client.get(\\\"/users/1\\\")\\n    \\n    @task(3)\\n    def create_user(self):\\n        self.client.post(\\n            \\\"/users\\\",\\n            json={\\\"name\\\": \\\"Test\\\", \\\"email\\\": \\\"test@example.com\\\"}\\n        )\\n```\\n\\n```bash\\n# 运行性能测试\\nlocust -f tests/performance/locustfile.py --host=http://localhost:8000\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 7. 部署指南\\n\\n### 7.1 Docker部署\\n\\n#### Dockerfile\\n```dockerfile\\n# Dockerfile\\nFROM python:3.11-slim\\n\\nWORKDIR /app\\n\\n# 安装系统依赖\\nRUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y \\\\\\n    gcc \\\\\\n    libpq-dev \\\\\\n    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*\\n\\n# 安装Python依赖\\nCOPY requirements.txt .\\nRUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt\\n\\n# 复制应用代码\\nCOPY . .\\n\\n# 暴露端口\\nEXPOSE 8000\\n\\n# 启动命令\\nCMD [\\\"uvicorn\\\", \\\"app.main:app\\\", \\\"--host\\\", \\\"0.0.0.0\\\", \\\"--port\\\", \\\"8000\\\", \\\"--workers\\\", \\\"4\\\"]\\n```\\n\\n#### docker-compose.yml\\n```yaml\\n# docker-compose.yml\\nversion: '3.8'\\n\\nservices:\\n  app:\\n    build: .\\n    ports:\\n      - \\\"8000:8000\\\"\\n    environment:\\n      - DATABASE_URL=postgresql+asyncpg://user:pass@db:5432/dbname\\n      - REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379/0\\n    depends_on:\\n      - db\\n      - redis\\n    restart: unless-stopped\\n\\n  db:\\n    image: postgres:15\\n    environment:\\n      - POSTGRES_USER=user\\n      - POSTGRES_PASSWORD=pass\\n      - POSTGRES_DB=dbname\\n    volumes:\\n      - postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data\\n    ports:\\n      - \\\"5432:5432\\\"\\n    restart: unless-stopped\\n\\n  redis:\\n    image: redis:7-alpine\\n    ports:\\n      - \\\"6379:6379\\\"\\n    volumes:\\n      - redis_data:/data\\n    restart: unless-stopped\\n\\n  worker:\\n    build: .\\n    command: [\\\"python\\\", \\\"app/tasks/worker.py\\\"]\\n    environment:\\n      - DATABASE_URL=postgresql+asyncpg://user:pass@db:5432/dbname\\n      - REDIS_URL=redis://redis:6379/0\\n    depends_on:\\n      - db\\n      - redis\\n    restart: unless-stopped\\n\\nvolumes:\\n  postgres_data:\\n  redis_data:\\n```\\n\\n### 7.2 Kubernetes部署\\n\\n#### deployment.yaml\\n```yaml\\n# deployment.yaml\\napiVersion: apps/v1\\nkind: Deployment\\nmetadata:\\n  name: async-app\\nspec:\\n  replicas: 3\\n  selector:\\n    matchLabels:\\n      app: async-app\\n  template:\\n    metadata:\\n      labels:\\n        app: async-app\\n    spec:\\n      containers:\\n      - name: app\\n        image: async-app:latest\\n        ports:\\n        - containerPort: 8000\\n        env:\\n        - name: DATABASE_URL\\n          valueFrom:\\n            secretKeyRef:\\n              name: app-secrets\\n              key: database-url\\n        - name: REDIS_URL\\n          valueFrom:\\n            secretKeyRef:\\n              name: app-secrets\\n              key: redis-url\\n        resources:\\n          requests:\\n            memory: \\\"256Mi\\\"\\n            cpu: \\\"250m\\\"\\n          limits:\\n            memory: \\\"512Mi\\\"\\n            cpu: \\\"500m\\\"\\n        livenessProbe:\\n          httpGet:\\n            path: /health\\n            port: 8000\\n          initialDelaySeconds: 30\\n          periodSeconds: 10\\n        readinessProbe:\\n          httpGet:\\n            path: /health\\n            port: 8000\\n          initialDelaySeconds: 5\\n          periodSeconds: 5\\n```\\n\\n### 7.3 CI/CD配置\\n\\n#### GitHub Actions\\n```yaml\\n# .github/workflows/ci.yml\\nname: CI\\n\\non:\\n  push:\\n    branches: [main]\\n  pull_request:\\n    branches: [main]\\n\\njobs:\\n  test:\\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\\n    \\n    services:\\n      postgres:\\n        image: postgres:15\\n        env:\\n          POSTGRES_USER: user\\n          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: pass\\n          POSTGRES_DB: testdb\\n        ports:\\n          - 5432:5432\\n        options: >-\\n          --health-cmd pg_isready\\n          --health-interval 10s\\n          --health-timeout 5s\\n          --health-retries 5\\n      \\n      redis:\\n        image: redis:7\\n        ports:\\n          - 6379:6379\\n        options: >-\\n          --health-cmd \\\"redis-cli ping\\\"\\n          --health-interval 10s\\n          --health-timeout 5s\\n          --health-retries 5\\n    \\n    steps:\\n    - uses: actions/checkout@v3\\n    \\n    - name: Set up Python\\n      uses: actions/setup-python@v4\\n      with:\\n        python-version: '3.11'\\n    \\n    - name: Install dependencies\\n      run: |\\n        pip install uv\\n        uv pip install -r requirements.txt\\n        uv pip install pytest pytest-asyncio httpx\\n    \\n    - name: Run tests\\n      env:\\n        DATABASE_URL: postgresql+asyncpg://user:pass@localhost:5432/testdb\\n        REDIS_URL: redis://localhost:6379/0\\n      run: |\\n        pytest tests/ -v\\n    \\n    - name: Build Docker image\\n      run: |\\n        docker build -t async-app:${{ github.sha }} .\\n    \\n    - name: Push to registry\\n      if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'\\n      run: |\\n        docker tag async-app:${{ github.sha }} registry.example.com/async-app:latest\\n        docker push registry.example.com/async-app:latest\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 8. 常见问题与解决方案\\n\\n### 8.1 性能问题\\n\\n#### 问题：数据库连接池耗尽\\n```\\n错误信息：asyncpg.exceptions.PoolTimeoutError: could not acquire a connection\\n```\\n\\n**解决方案**：\\n```python\\n# 1. 增加连接池大小\\npool = await asyncpg.create_pool(\\n    dsn=DATABASE_URL,\\n    min_size=10,\\n    max_size=50  # 增加最大连接数\\n)\\n\\n# 2. 检查是否有连接泄漏\\nasync def get_user(user_id: int):\\n    async with db.get_connection().acquire() as conn:  # 使用async with确保连接释放\\n        row = await conn.fetchrow(\\\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = $1\\\", user_id)\\n        return dict(row) if row else None\\n\\n# 3. 监控连接池状态\\nasync def monitor_pool():\\n    while True:\\n        size = len(db.pool._pool) if db.pool else 0\\n        logger.info(f\\\"Pool size: {size}\\\")\\n        await asyncio.sleep(60)\\n```\\n\\n#### 问题：任务队列积压\\n```\\n现象：任务执行缓慢，队列积压严重\\n```\\n\\n**解决方案**：\\n```python\\n# 1. 增加Worker数量\\nworker = Worker(broker=broker, worker_timeout=300_000, worker_concurrency=4)\\n\\n# 2. 优化任务执行时间\\n@app.actor\\ndef optimized_task(data: dict):\\n    # 避免在任务中做耗时操作\\n    # 将耗时操作拆分为多个小任务\\n    pass\\n\\n# 3. 使用任务优先级\\n@app.actor(priority=10)  # 高优先级\\ndef important_task(data: dict):\\n    pass\\n\\n@app.actor(priority=1)  # 低优先级\\ndef background_task(data: dict):\\n    pass\\n```\\n\\n### 8.2 调试问题\\n\\n#### 问题：异步代码调试困难\\n```\\n现象：异步代码难以设置断点，调试困难\\n```\\n\\n**解决方案**：\\n```python\\n# 1. 使用pytest-asyncio调试\\nimport pytest\\n\\n@pytest.mark.asyncio\\nasync def test_debug():\\n    breakpoint()  # 设置断点\\n    result = await some_async_function()\\n    assert result == expected\\n\\n# 2. 使用logging调试\\nimport logging\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nasync def debug_function():\\n    logger.debug(f\\\"Entering function with args: {args}\\\")\\n    result = await some_async_function()\\n    logger.debug(f\\\"Result: {result}\\\")\\n    return result\\n\\n# 3. 使用asyncio调试模式\\nimport asyncio\\nasyncio.run(main(), debug=True)\\n```\\n\\n### 8.3 部署问题\\n\\n#### 问题：Docker容器启动失败\\n```\\n错误信息：ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'app'\\n```\\n\\n**解决方案**：\\n```dockerfile\\n# Dockerfile\\nFROM python:3.11-slim\\n\\nWORKDIR /app\\n\\n# 复制requirements.txt\\nCOPY requirements.txt .\\n\\n# 安装依赖\\nRUN pip install --no-cache-dir -r requirements.txt\\n\\n# 复制应用代码\\nCOPY . .\\n\\n# 确保__init__.py存在\\nRUN touch app/__init__.py\\n\\n# 启动命令\\nCMD [\\\"uvicorn\\\", \\\"app.main:app\\\", \\\"--host\\\", \\\"0.0.0.0\\\", \\\"--port\\\", \\\"8000\\\"]\\n```\\n\\n#### 问题：数据库连接失败\\n```\\n错误信息：asyncpg.exceptions.ConnectionError: could not connect to server\\n```\\n\\n**解决方案**：\\n```yaml\\n# docker-compose.yml\\nservices:\\n  app:\\n    depends_on:\\n      db:\\n        condition: service_healthy  # 等待数据库健康检查通过\\n\\n  db:\\n    healthcheck:\\n      test: [\\\"CMD-SHELL\\\", \\\"pg_isready -U user\\\"]\\n      interval: 5s\\n      timeout: 5s\\n      retries: 5\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 总结\\n\\n本实施指南提供了Python Web异步编程技术栈的完整实施路径，包括：\\n\\n1. **环境准备**：Python版本、依赖管理工具\\n2. **技术栈搭建**：FastAPI、asyncpg、Dramatiq的完整配置\\n3. **代码规范**：异步编程、数据库操作、任务队列的最佳实践\\n4. **性能优化**：数据库、应用、服务器的性能优化策略\\n5. **监控运维**：健康检查、日志、任务队列监控\\n6. **测试策略**：单元测试、集成测试、性能测试\\n7. **部署指南**：Docker、Kubernetes、CI/CD配置\\n8. **问题解决**：常见性能、调试、部署问题的解决方案\\n\\n**实施建议**：\\n1. 从简单项目开始，逐步掌握异步编程\\n2. 遵循代码规范和最佳实践\\n3. 建立完善的测试和监控体系\\n4. 持续优化性能和运维流程\\n\\n---\\n\\n*报告版本：V4.0* **步骤4: writetext**
- 参数: `{"path": "E:\\test_dir\\temp_analysis.py", "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"临时分析脚本 - 分析test.txt中的日志调用\"\"\"\nimport re\nfrom collections import Counter, defaultdict\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\nfile_path = r\"E:\\test_dir\\test.txt\"\n\nwith open(file_path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n    lines = f.readlines()\n\ntotal_lines = len(lines)\n\n# 阶段一：清洗解析 - 识别日志级别行\nlevels = {\"ERROR\": [], \"WARNING\": [], \"INFO\": [], \"DEBUG\": []}\npattern_map = {\n    \"ERROR\": r\"logger\\.error\\s*\\(\",\n    \"WARNING\": r\"logger\\.warning\\s*\\(\",\n    \"INFO\": r\"logger\\.info\\s*\\(\",\n    \"DEBUG\": r\"logger\\.debug\\s*\\(\",\n}\n\nfor i, line in enumerate(lines, 1):\n    stripped = line.strip()\n    if not stripped or stripped.startswith(\"#\"):\n        continue\n    for level, pattern in pattern_map.items():\n        if re.search(pattern, line):\n            levels[level].append({\"line_num\": i, \"content\": stripped})\n            break\n\n# 阶段二：统计分析\nlevel_counts = {k: len(v) for k, v in levels.items()}\ntotal_log_lines = sum(level_counts.values())\nlevel_percentages = {}\nfor k, v in level_counts.items():\n    level_percentages[k] = round(v / total_log_lines * 100, 2) if total_log_lines > 0 else 0\n\nerror_warn_ratio = round((level_counts[\"ERROR\"] + level_counts[\"WARNING\"]) / total_log_lines * 100, 2) if total_log_lines > 0 else 0\n\n# 严重度评分 (0-100, 越高越严重)\n# 基于: ERROR权重5, WARNING权重3, INFO权重1\nif total_log_lines > 0:\n    severity_score = min(100, round(\n        (level_counts[\"ERROR\"] * 5 + level_counts[\"WARNING\"] * 3 + level_counts[\"INFO\"] * 1) / total_log_lines * 20, 1\n    ))\nelse:\n    severity_score = 0\n\n# 去重统计\nunique_messages = {}\nfor level, entries in levels.items():\n    unique_msgs = set(e[\"content\"] for e in entries)\n    unique_messages[level] = list(unique_msgs)\n\n# 阶段三：时间序列分析 - 提取时间戳\ntimestamp_pattern = r\"(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}\\s+\\d{2}:\\d{2}:\\d{2})\"\nhourly_distribution = defaultdict(int)\nhas_timestamps = False\nall_timestamps = []\n\nfor level, entries in levels.items():\n    for entry in entries:\n        match = re.search(timestamp_pattern, entry[\"content\"])\n        if match:\n            has_timestamps = True\n            ts = match.group(1)\n            all_timestamps.append(ts)\n            hour = ts.split()[1].split(\":\")[0] if \" \" in ts else \"unknown\"\n            hourly_distribution[hour] += 1\n\n# 阶段四：模式聚合分析 - TOP5错误和警告\nerror_patterns = Counter()\nwarn_patterns = Counter()\n\nfor entry in levels[\"ERROR\"]:\n    # 提取错误消息主体（去除变量部分）\n    msg = re.sub(r'\\{[^}]*\\}', '{}', entry[\"content\"])\n    msg = re.sub(r'f\".*?\"', 'f-string', msg)\n    error_patterns[msg] += 1\n\nfor entry in levels[\"WARNING\"]:\n    msg = re.sub(r'\\{[^}]*\\}', '{}', entry[\"content\"])\n    warn_patterns[msg] += 1\n\ntop5_errors = error_patterns.most_common(5)\ntop5_warnings = warn_patterns.most_common(5)\n\n# 根因分析\nerror_root_causes = []\nfor msg, count in top5_errors:\n    cause = \"未知\"\n    if \"assert\" in msg.lower() or \"应为\" in msg:\n        cause = \"断言失败 - 返回值不符合预期\"\n    elif \"类型\" in msg or \"instanceof\" in msg.lower():\n        cause = \"类型检查失败\"\n    elif \"空\" in msg or \"None\" in msg:\n        cause = \"空值/None引用\"\n    elif \"超时\" in msg or \"timeout\" in msg.lower():\n        cause = \"操作超时\"\n    elif \"连接\" in msg or \"connect\" in msg.lower():\n        cause = \"网络连接问题\"\n    else:\n        cause = f\"异常捕获: {msg[:80]}\"\n    error_root_causes.append({\n        \"message\": msg,\n        \"count\": count,\n        \"root_cause\": cause\n    })\n\nwarn_root_causes = []\nfor msg, count in top5_warnings:\n    cause = \"一般警告\"\n    if \"失败\" in msg or \"failed\" in msg.lower():\n        cause = \"操作失败告警\"\n    elif \"未\" in msg or \"not found\" in msg.lower():\n        cause = \"资源未找到\"\n    elif \"配置\" in msg or \"config\" in msg.lower():\n        cause = \"配置相关警告\"\n    else:\n        cause = f\"运行时警告: {msg[:80]}\"\n    warn_root_causes.append({\n        \"message\": msg,\n        \"count\": count,\n        \"root_cause\": cause\n    })\n\n# 构建结果\nresult = {\n    \"summary\": {\n        \"total_lines\": total_lines,\n        \"total_log_lines\": total_log_lines,\n        \"level_counts\": level_counts,\n        \"level_percentages\": level_percentages,\n        \"error_warn_ratio\": error_warn_ratio,\n        \"severity_score\": severity_score,\n        \"has_timestamps\": has_timestamps,\n        \"timestamp_count\": len(all_timestamps)\n    },\n    \"hourly_distribution\": dict(sorted(hourly_distribution.items())),\n    \"unique_counts\": {k: len(v) for k, v in unique_messages.items()},\n    \"top5_errors\": [{\"message\": m, \"count\": c} for m, c in top5_errors],\n    \"top5_warnings\": [{\"message\": m, \"count\": c} for m, c in top5_warnings],\n    \"error_root_causes\": error_root_causes,\n    \"warn_root_causes\": warn_root_causes,\n    \"details\": {\n        \"errors\": [{\"line\": e[\"line_num\"], \"content\": e[\"content\"]} for e in levels[\"ERROR\"]],\n        \"warnings\": [{\"line\": e[\"line_num\"], \"content\": e[\"content\"]} for e in levels[\"WARNING\"]],\n        \"infos\": [{\"line\": e[\"line_num\"], \"content\": e[\"content\"]} for e in levels[\"INFO\"]],\n        \"debugs\": [{\"line\": e[\"line_num\"], \"content\": e[\"content\"]} for e in levels[\"DEBUG\"]]\n    }\n}\n\nimport json\nprint(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))"}`

**步骤7: writetext**
- 参数: `{"path": "E:\\test_dir\\log_analyzer.py", "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-\n\"\"\"\nlog_analyzer.py - 日志分析工具 v1.0\n====================================\n支持命令行参数分析日志文件，输出JSON格式分析结果。\n\n作者: 欧阳\n创建时间: 2026-07-18\n\n用法:\n    python log_analyzer.py -i input.log -o ./output\n    python log_analyzer.py -i input.log --level ERROR --top 10\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport re\nimport sys\nfrom collections import Counter, defaultdict\nfrom datetime import datetime\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# 类型定义\n# ============================================================\n\nclass LogEntry:\n    \"\"\"单条日志条目\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, line_num: int, level: str, content: str, timestamp: Optional[str] = None):\n        self.line_num = line_num\n        self.level = level\n        self.content = content\n        self.timestamp = timestamp\n\n\nclass AnalysisResult:\n    \"\"\"分析结果容器\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.summary: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n        self.hourly_distribution: Dict[str, int] = {}\n        self.unique_counts: Dict[str, int] = {}\n        self.top_errors: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n        self.top_warnings: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n        self.error_root_causes: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n        self.warn_root_causes: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n        self.entries: Dict[str, List[LogEntry]] = defaultdict(list)\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# 核心分析函数\n# ============================================================\n\ndef parse_log_line(line: str) -> Optional[Tuple[str, Optional[str]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    解析单行日志，返回(级别, 时间戳)或None\n    \n    Args:\n        line: 原始日志行\n        \n    Returns:\n        (level, timestamp) 元组，无法解析则返回None\n    \"\"\"\n    line_stripped = line.strip()\n    \n    # 跳过空行和纯注释\n    if not line_stripped or line_stripped.startswith(\"#\"):\n        return None\n    \n    # 匹配常见日志格式: [2026-07-18 01:39:39] ERROR message\n    ts_pattern = r\"(\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}[\\sT]\\d{2}:\\d{2}:\\d{2})\"\n    timestamp_match = re.search(ts_pattern, line_stripped)\n    timestamp = timestamp_match.group(1) if timestamp_match else None\n    \n    # 提取级别\n    level_map = {\n        \"ERROR\": r\"\\berror\\b\",\n        \"WARNING\": r\"\\bwarn(?:ing)?\\b\",\n        \"INFO\": r\"\\binfo\\b\",\n        \"DEBUG\": r\"\\bdebug\\b\",\n    }\n    \n    for level, pattern in level_map.items():\n        if re.search(pattern, line_stripped, re.IGNORECASE):\n            return (level, timestamp)\n    \n    return None\n\n\ndef clean_and_parse(\n    lines: List[str],\n    min_level: Optional[str] = None\n) -> List[LogEntry]:\n    \"\"\"\n    清洗并解析日志行\n    \n    Args:\n        lines: 原始日志行列表\n        min_level: 最低级别过滤 (DEBUG < INFO < WARNING < ERROR)\n        \n    Returns:\n        解析后的日志条目列表\n    \"\"\"\n    level_priority = {\"DEBUG\": 0, \"INFO\": 1, \"WARNING\": 2, \"ERROR\": 3}\n    min_priority = level_priority.get(min_level, 0) if min_level else 0\n    \n    entries: List[LogEntry] = []\n    for i, line in enumerate(lines, 1):\n        result = parse_log_line(line)\n        if result is None:\n            continue\n        \n        level, timestamp = result\n        if level_priority.get(level, 0) >= min_priority:\n            entries.append(LogEntry(i, level, line.strip(), timestamp))\n    \n    return entries\n\n\ndef analyze_levels(entries: List[LogEntry]) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"统计各级别出现次数\"\"\"\n    counts: Dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)\n    for entry in entries:\n        counts[entry.level] += 1\n    return dict(counts)\n\n\ndef calculate_severity(level_counts: Dict[str, int], total: int) -> float:\n    \"\"\"\n    计算严重度评分 (0-100)\n    \n    权重: ERROR=5, WARNING=3, INFO=1, DEBUG=0.5\n    \"\"\"\n    if total == 0:\n        return 0.0\n    \n    weights = {\"ERROR\": 5, \"WARNING\": 3, \"INFO\": 1, \"DEBUG\": 0.5}\n    score = sum(level_counts.get(level, 0) * weight \n                for level, weight in weights.items())\n    return min(100.0, round(score / total * 20, 1))\n\n\ndef extract_hour(timestamp_str: Optional[str]) -> Optional[str]:\n    \"\"\"从时间戳提取小时\"\"\"\n    if not timestamp_str:\n        return None\n    match = re.search(r\"(\\d{2}):\\d{2}:\\d{2}\", timestamp_str)\n    return match.group(1) if match else None\n\n\ndef cluster_messages(\n    entries: List[LogEntry],\n    top_n: int = 5\n) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    对相同消息聚类，返回TOP N高频模式\n    \n    Args:\n        entries: 日志条目列表\n        top_n: 返回数量\n        \n    Returns:\n        [{\"message\": \"...\", \"count\": N}, ...]\n    \"\"\"\n    patterns = Counter()\n    for entry in entries:\n        # 标准化消息：去除变量部分\n        msg = re.sub(r\"\\{[^}]*\\}\", \"{}\", entry.content)\n        msg = re.sub(r\"f['\\\"].*?['\\\"]\", \"f-string\", msg)\n        patterns[msg] += 1\n    \n    return [{\"message\": m, \"count\": c} for m, c in patterns.most_common(top_n)]\n\n\ndef analyze_root_cause(message: str, level: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"分析错误的根因模式\"\"\"\n    lower_msg = message.lower()\n    \n    if level == \"ERROR\":\n        if \"assert\" in lower_msg or \"应为\" in message:\n            return \"断言失败 - 返回值不符合预期\"\n        elif \"类型\" in message or \"instanceof\" in lower_msg:\n            return \"类型检查失败\"\n        elif \"空\" in message or \"none\" in lower_msg:\n            return \"空值/None引用\"\n        elif \"超时\" in message or \"timeout\" in lower_msg:\n            return \"操作超时\"\n        elif \"连接\" in message or \"connect\" in lower_msg:\n            return \"网络连接问题\"\n        elif \"test\" in lower_msg and (\"fail\" in lower_msg or \"失败\" in message):\n            return \"测试用例执行异常\"\n        else:\n            return f\"异常捕获: {message[:80]}\"\n    \n    elif level == \"WARNING\":\n        if \"失败\" in message or \"failed\" in lower_msg:\n            return \"操作失败告警\"\n        elif \"未\" in message or \"not found\" in lower_msg:\n            return \"资源未找到\"\n        elif \"配置\" in message or \"config\" in lower_msg:\n            return \"配置相关警告\"\n        else:\n            return f\"运行时警告: {message[:80]}\"\n    \n    return \"未知\"\n\n\ndef build_analysis_result(\n    entries: List[LogEntry],\n    total_lines: int,\n    top_n: int = 5\n) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"构建完整分析结果\"\"\"\n    level_counts = analyze_levels(entries)\n    total_log = len(entries)\n    \n    # 百分比\n    percentages = {}\n    for level, count in level_counts.items():\n        percentages[level] = round(count / total_log * 100, 2) if total_log > 0 else 0\n    \n    # Error+Warning比例\n    error_warn_count = level_counts.get(\"ERROR\", 0) + level_counts.get(\"WARNING\", 0)\n    error_warn_ratio = round(error_warn_count / total_log * 100, 2) if total_log > 0 else 0\n    \n    # 严重度\n    severity = calculate_severity(level_counts, total_log)\n    \n    # 时间分布\n    hourly_dist: Dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)\n    timestamps = []\n    for entry in entries:\n        hour = extract_hour(entry.timestamp)\n        if hour:\n            hourly_dist[hour] += 1\n            timestamps.append(entry.timestamp)\n    \n    # 去重统计\n    unique_counts = {}\n    for level in [\"ERROR\", \"WARNING\", \"INFO\", \"DEBUG\"]:\n        level_entries = [e for e in entries if e.level == level]\n        unique_msgs = set(e.content for e in level_entries)\n        unique_counts[level] = len(unique_msgs)\n    \n    # TOP错误和警告\n    errors = [e for e in entries if e.level == \"ERROR\"]\n    warnings = [e for e in entries if e.level == \"WARNING\"]\n    \n    top_errors = cluster_messages(errors, top_n)\n    top_warnings = cluster_messages(warnings, top_n)\n    \n    # 根因分析\n    error_causes = []\n    for item in top_errors:\n        cause = analyze_root_cause(item[\"message\"], \"ERROR\")\n        error_causes.append({\n            \"message\": item[\"message\"],\n            \"count\": item[\"count\"],\n            \"root_cause\": cause\n        })\n    \n    warn_causes = []\n    for item in top_warnings:\n        cause = analyze_root_cause(item[\"message\"], \"WARNING\")\n        warn_causes.append({\n            \"message\": item[\"message\"],\n            \"count\": item[\"count\"],\n            \"root_cause\": cause\n        })\n    \n    return {\n        \"summary\": {\n            \"total_lines\": total_lines,\n            \"total_log_lines\": total_log,\n            \"level_counts\": level_counts,\n            \"level_percentages\": percentages,\n            \"error_warn_ratio\": error_warn_ratio,\n            \"severity_score\": severity,\n            \"has_timestamps\": len(timestamps) > 0,\n            \"timestamp_count\": len(timestamps)\n        },\n        \"hourly_distribution\": dict(sorted(hourly_dist.items())),\n        \"unique_counts\": unique_counts,\n        \"top5_errors\": top_errors,\n        \"top5_warnings\": top_warnings,\n        \"error_root_causes\": error_causes,\n        \"warn_root_causes\": warn_causes,\n        \"analysis_time\": datetime.now().isoformat()\n    }\n\n\n# ============================================================\n# 主程序\n# ============================================================\n\ndef parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:\n    \"\"\"解析命令行参数\"\"\"\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"日志分析工具 - 分析日志文件并输出JSON格式结果\",\n        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,\n        epilog=\"\"\"\n示例:\n  python log_analyzer.py -i app.log\n  python log_analyzer.py -i app.log -o ./reports\n  python log_analyzer.py -i app.log --level WARNING --top 10\n        \"\"\"\n    )\n    \n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"-i\", \"--input\",\n        required=True,\n        help=\"输入日志文件路径\"\n    )\n    \n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"-o\", \"--output-dir\",\n        default=None,\n        help=\"输出目录路径（默认输出到终端）\"\n    )\n    \n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--level\",\n        choices=[\"DEBUG\", \"INFO\", \"WARNING\", \"ERROR\"],\n        default=None,\n        help=\"按级别筛选日志（只显示该级别及更高级别）\"\n    )\n    \n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--top\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5,\n        help=\"TOP N数量，默认5\"\n    )\n    \n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--format\",\n        choices=[\"json\", \"text\"],\n        default=\"json\",\n        help=\"输出格式，默认json\"\n    )\n    \n    return parser.parse_args()\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    \"\"\"主入口\"\"\"\n    args = parse_args()\n    \n    # 读取文件\n    input_path = Path(args.input)\n    if not input_path.exists():\n        print(f\"错误: 文件不存在 - {args.input}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        sys.exit(1)\n    \n    with open(input_path, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        raw_lines = f.readlines()\n    \n    print(f\"[LOG_ANALYZER] 开始分析: {args.input}\")\n    print(f\"[LOG_ANALYZER] 总行数: {len(raw_lines)}\")\n    \n    # 清洗解析\n    entries = clean_and_parse(raw_lines, args.level)\n    print(f\"[LOG_ANALYZER] 有效日志行: {len(entries)}\")\n    \n    # 分析\n    result = build_analysis_result(entries, len(raw_lines), args.top)\n    \n    # 输出\n    if args.output_dir:\n        output_path = Path(args.output_dir)\n        output_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        \n        # 生成带时间戳的文件名\n        timestamp = datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y%m%d_%H%M%S\")\n        \n        json_file = output_path / f\"log_analysis_{timestamp}.json\"\n        with open(json_file, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            json.dump(result, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n        print(f\"[LOG_ANALYZER] JSON结果已保存: {json_file}\")\n        \n        if args.format == \"text\":\n            txt_file = output_path / f\"log_analysis_{timestamp}.txt\"\n            with open(txt_file, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n                f.write(generate_text_report(result))\n            print(f\"[LOG_ANALYZER] 文本报告已保存: {txt_file}\")\n    else:\n        print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))\n    \n    print(\"[LOG_ANALYZER] 分析完成\")\n\n\ndef generate_text_report(result: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"生成文本格式报告\"\"\"\n    lines = []\n    s = result[\"summary\"]\n    \n    lines.append(\"=\" * 60)\n    lines.append(\"           日志分析报告\")\n    lines.append(\"=\" * 60)\n    lines.append(\"\")\n    lines.append(f\"分析时间: {result.get('analysis_time', 'N/A')}\")\n    lines.append(f\"总行数:   {s['total_lines']}\")\n    lines.append(f\"日志行数: {s['total_log_lines']}\")\n    lines.append(f\"严重度:   {s['severity_score']}/100\")\n    lines.append(\"\")\n    \n    lines.append(\"--- 级别分布 ---\")\n    for level, pct in s[\"level_percentages\"].items():\n        count = s[\"level_counts\"].get(level, 0)\n        lines.append(f\"  {level}: {count} ({pct}%)\")\n    \n    lines.append(\"\")\n    lines.append(f\"Error+Warning占比: {s['error_warn_ratio']}%\")\n    \n    if result.get(\"top5_errors\"):\n        lines.append(\"\")\n        lines.append(\"--- TOP 错误 ---\")\n        for i, err in enumerate(result[\"top5_errors\"], 1):\n            lines.append(f\"  {i}. [{err['count']}x] {err['message']}\")\n    \n    if result.get(\"top5_warnings\"):\n        lines.append(\"\")\n        lines.append(\"--- TOP 警告 ---\")\n        for i, warn in enumerate(result[\"top5_warnings\"], 1):\n            lines.append(f\"  {i}. [{warn['count']}x] {warn['message']}\")\n    \n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()"}`

{e}\")\n            → 单元测试 display_item() 异常捕获\n  \n  行号 296: logger.error(f\"✗ 测试4失败：配置类 - {e}\")\n            → 单元测试 配置类验证异常捕获\n  \n  行号 305: logger.error(f\"✗ 测试5失败：日志功能 - {e}\")\n            → 单元测试 日志功能验证异常捕获\n\n  注意: 以上ERROR均为try-except块中的异常捕获语句，表示在测试执行时可能遇到的\n        异常情况。实际运行时若测试通过，这些错误不会输出。\n\n2.4 WARNING级别详细记录\n\n  行号 312: logger.warning(f\"测试完成，{failures}个测试失败\")\n            → 测试汇总警告，当测试用例有失败时触发\n\n2.5 INFO级别详细记录\n\n  行号 256: logger.info(\"✓ 测试1通过：get_content\")\n  行号 267: logger.info(\"✓ 测试2通过：get_app_info\")\n  行号 283: logger.info(\"✓ 测试3通过：display_item\")\n  行号 294: logger.info(\"✓ 测试4通过：配置类\")\n  行号 303: logger.info(\"✓ 测试5通过：日志功能\")\n  行号 310: logger.info(f\"所有测试通过！\")\n  行号 326: logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n  行号 327: logger.info(\"测试程序启动\")\n  行号 328: logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n  行号 335: logger.info(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n  行号 336: logger.info(\"运行单元测试\")\n  行号 337: logger.info(\"=\" * 60)\n\n================================================================================\n三、统计分析\n================================================================================\n\n3.1 各级别统计\n\n  级别      出现次数    占比        去重后数量\n  ─────────────────────────────────────────\n  ERROR       5         27.78%      5 (全部唯一)\n  WARNING     1          5.56%      1\n  INFO       12         66.67%      10 (2条重复分隔线)\n  DEBUG       0          0.00%      0\n\n3.2 严重度评估\n\n  Error+Warning 占总日志数比例: 33.33%\n  \n  严重度评分计算模型:\n    - ERROR权重: 5分/次\n    - WARNING权重: 3分/次\n    - INFO权重: 1分/次\n    - 归一化到0-100分\n  \n  当前严重度评分: 44.4 / 100\n  \n  评分解读:\n    [0-20]  低风险 - 系统运行正常\n    [21-40] 中低风险 - 偶发问题\n    [41-60] 中等风险 - 需要关注\n    [61-80] 中高风险 - 需要处理\n    [81-100]高风险 - 紧急处理\n\n  结论: 当前代码库的日志严重度处于中等偏低水平，主要来源于测试框架的异常\n        捕获机制。实际运行时若测试通过，ERROR级别日志不会出现。\n\n3.3 信息密度分析\n\n  - 代码总行数: 341行\n  - 日志调用行数: 18行\n  - 日志信息密度: 5.28% (属于正常范围)\n  - 建议: 日志覆盖率合理，INFO级别主要用于流程跟踪，ERROR/WARNING用于异常处理\n\n================================================================================\n四、时间序列分析\n================================================================================\n\n4.1 时间戳提取结果\n\n  检测到的时间戳: 0个\n  原因说明: test.txt为源代码文件，其中的logger调用不包含运行时时间戳信息。\n           日志时间戳仅在程序实际运行时由logging模块自动生成。\n\n4.2 小时分布\n\n  由于未检测到时间戳信息，无法按小时维度统计日志分布密度。\n  如需进行时间序列分析，建议使用程序实际运行产生的日志文件。\n\n4.3 峰值分析\n\n  不适用 - 源代码文件无时间维度信息。\n\n================================================================================\n五、模式聚合分析\n================================================================================\n\n5.1 TOP5 最频繁错误模式\n\n  排名  消息模式                          出现次数  根因分析\n  ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  1     logger.error(f-string)              5        测试异常捕获模板\n                                     5次      所有ERROR均源自run_tests()函数中\n                                             的try-except块，用于捕获各测试\n                                             用例执行时的异常。实际运行时若\n                                             测试通过，这些日志不会输出。\n  \n  根因总结:\n    - 模式类型: 异常处理模板\n    - 影响范围: run_tests() 函数内5个测试用例\n    - 风险等级: 低 (仅测试失败时触发)\n    - 建议: 无需修改，这是标准的异常处理模式\n\n5.2 TOP5 最频繁警告模式\n\n  排名  消息模式                              出现次数  根因分析\n  ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  1     logger.warning(\"测试完成，{failures}个...\")  1    测试汇总告警\n                                             当run_tests()返回的failures > 0时触发\n  \n  根因总结:\n    - 模式类型: 测试状态汇总\n    - 触发条件: failures > 0\n    - 风险等级: 低\n\n5.3 高频错误根因分析\n\n  错误类别: 单元测试异常捕获\n  \n  根因模式:\n    1. 所有ERROR均位于run_tests()函数的try-except块中\n    2. 采用统一的错误日志格式: \"✗ 测试N失败：{测试名} - {异常信息}\"\n    3. 错误消息中的变量部分(e)会在运行时动态填充\n  \n  优化建议:\n    - 当前模式合理，符合Python标准异常处理实践\n    - 可考虑将错误消息模板抽取为常量，便于统一修改\n    - 建议添加更多上下文信息(如模块名、堆栈追踪)\n\n================================================================================\n六、综合结论与建议\n================================================================================\n\n6.1 总体评价\n\n  文件类型: Python源代码文件 (非标准日志文件)\n  日志覆盖: 良好 (INFO/WARNING/ERROR三级齐全，缺少DEBUG)\n  代码质量: 优秀 (结构化清晰，含完整测试框架)\n  严重程度: 中等偏低 (44.4/100)\n\n6.2 关键发现\n\n  1. test.txt是一个完整的Python测试程序，包含单元测试框架\n  2. 日志调用集中在run_tests()函数和main入口点\n  3. ERROR级别日志为异常捕获模板，实际运行时不一定触发\n  4. 缺少DEBUG级别日志，调试信息不足\n  5. 无时间戳信息，无法进行时间序列分析\n\n6.3 改进建议\n\n  [高优先级]\n  - 建议在实际部署时使用运行时日志文件进行分析，而非源代码文件\n  \n  [中优先级]\n  - 可在关键函数中添加logger.debug()调试日志\n  - 建议添加日志轮转和持久化配置\n  \n  [低优先级]\n  - 可考虑统一错误消息格式模板\n\n6.4 分析产物清单\n\n  ├── log_analyzer.py          (可复用分析脚本)\n  ├── analysis_report.txt      (本报告 - 人工阅读)\n  ├── analysis_report.json     (JSON格式汇总数据)\n  └── analysis_report_summary.md (管理者摘要报告)\n\n================================================================================\n                        报告结束\n              生成时间: 2026-07-18 01:39:39\n================================================================================\n"}`
